The evidence mixes an older longitudinal programme, recent scientific systems/operational observations and new AI/low-code audits and controlled prototype studies. They are neither equivalent endpoints nor independent replications of one proposition.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Decision or consumer</dt><dd><p>Result consumer, recovery decision-maker and reviewer.</p></dd><dt>Actor and authority boundary</dt><dd><p>An observer need not have authority to act, and a model’s certainty cannot replace a participant’s legitimate judgement.</p></dd><dt>Communication or interaction requirement</dt><dd><p>State whether evidence concerns a recorded event, a communicated commitment or an observed consequence.</p></dd><dt>Observable postcondition</dt><dd><p>The claim’s evidence boundary is visible and no external success is asserted solely from an inadequate internal event.</p></dd><dt>Known failure modes</dt><dd><ol><li><p>A complete log is mistaken for complete reality, including manual work or downstream outcomes.</p></li><li><p>Observation can be costly, intrusive, stale or circular when it merely repeats the same source.</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Important criticisms</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/16.md#r-1686243f93b5441633db0a162c5598dc0703407f9ce2a332e477ff070fa55cf2">Complete shared IMPORTANT CRITICISMS · 1686243f93b5</a></p></dd><dt>How the property evolved</dt><dd><p>REFINED: observability is coupled to claim-relative external correspondence rather than total surveillance.</p></dd><dt>Mature or evolved form</dt><dd><p>Identify the observation boundary and connect consequential claims to an appropriate external or participant source; preserve uncertainty when correspondence cannot be checked. Strongly retain the distinction; no general demand for omniscient observation.</p></dd><dt>Ceremony vs property</dt><dd><dl><dt>Protected function</dt><dd><p>A complete log is mistaken for complete reality, including manual work or downstream outcomes.</p></dd><dt>Minimal adequate alternative</dt><dd><p>A trustworthy participant report is sufficient when the consequence and risk justify it; independent sensing is not universal.</p></dd><dt>Fuller form trigger</dt><dd><p>Completion, conformance, recovery or effectiveness is inferred from internal history.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Result consumer, recovery decision-maker and reviewer.</p></dd><dt>Cost of simplification</dt><dd><p>Removing the mechanism is acceptable only while the cheap path still establishes this claim: The claim’s evidence boundary is visible and no external success is asserted solely from an inadequate internal event.</p></dd><dt>Retirement or omission</dt><dd><p>Retire or omit the extra machinery when the trigger, consumer or relevant dependency disappears, after settling/transferring the outstanding work or evidence that still relies on it. A trustworthy participant report is sufficient when the consequence and risk justify it; independent sensing is not universal.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Current status</dt><dd><p>STRONGLY_RETAINED</p></dd><dt>Property kind</dt><dd><p>CORRECTNESS_OR_EVIDENCE_CRITERION</p></dd><dt>Evidence strength partitions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/b0.md#r-b0607b4ca4e3e62e403108cbb48273b23be13fa30a4349ba0dd60171a467ebd4">Complete shared EVIDENCE STRENGTH PARTITIONS · b0607b4ca4e3</a></p></dd><dt>Primary source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-S009</p></li><li><p>EWM-S041</p></li><li><p>EWM-S045</p></li><li><p>EWM-S050</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Critical source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-S031</p></li><li><p>EWM-S049</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Empirical or case source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-S031</p></li><li><p>EWM-S049</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Contrary evidence</dt><dd><dl><dt>Source criticism ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-C009</p></li><li><p>EWM-C019</p></li><li><p>EWM-C020</p></li><li><p>EWM-C022</p></li><li><p>EWM-C026</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-S031</p></li><li><p>EWM-S049</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Analytical objection</dt><dd><p>Observation can be costly, intrusive, stale or circular when it merely repeats the same source.</p></dd><dt>Response and remaining limit</dt><dd><p>Strongly retain the distinction; no general demand for omniscient observation.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Assumption sensitivity</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/b2.md#r-b2b0db4ab05f0d52e3db3473f3a3fd2db707405cd61749759376c8a95aa5216c">Complete shared ASSUMPTION SENSITIVITY · b2b0db4ab05f</a></p></dd><dt>Composition relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-R038</p></li><li><p>EWM-R040</p></li><li><p>EWM-R042</p></li><li><p>EWM-R043</p></li><li><p>EWM-R046</p></li><li><p>EWM-R055</p></li><li><p>EWM-R062</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Duplicate or supersession links</dt><dd><ol><li><dl><dt>Relation id</dt><dd><p>EWM-R055</p></dd><dt>Role</dt><dd><p>STRONGER_FORM</p></dd></dl></li></ol></dd><dt>Open questions</dt><dd><ol><li><p>When are two corroborating records actually copies of one fallible observation?</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Disposition rationale</dt><dd><p>Strongly retain the distinction; ## EWM:EWM-064

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-014.md#EWM-EWM-064">EWM:EWM-064</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EWM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EWM-064</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Workflow restricted universally to a serial pipeline or DAG</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/a4.md#r-a400d2a80e2a23b8af8db0e84aeb6fef06b24b08c8c5122488c85485f9cfd6bc">Complete shared origin class · a400d2a80e2a</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_WITH_SHARED_ANCESTRY_AND_DOCUMENTED_INTERSECTIONS</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/89.md#r-89e422f2aa3bef339ffc65e45e62844e29ebdf89306500f62ae278105c64ca9c">Complete shared evidence limit · 89e422f2aa3b</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Workflow control-flow, activation scopes, concurrency, repeated work and arbitration</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation;
